# violating-CI — every message a judge called a direct disclosure

Generated by `experiments/agent5/disclosure_ci_report.py`. One entry per message that **either** judge called `direct`, across the none / standard / strict confidentiality cells. Each entry carries the full message and both judges' verdict, quoted span and reason, so a disagreement is visible rather than pooled away. Rates are in `violating-CI.md`.

`AGREED` in a heading means both judges said direct; otherwise only the judge named said so.
